# 18.29 — View API

Backend:

```text
POST /api/feed/<post_id>/view/
```

Add:

```python
from rest_framework import status
from .models import FeedInteraction


class FeedPostViewView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [
        IsAuthenticated,
    ]

    def post(self, request, post_id):
        FeedInteraction.objects.create(
            user=request.user,
            post_id=post_id,
            interaction_type=(
                FeedInteraction
                .InteractionType
                .VIEW
            ),
        )

        return Response(
            {"success": True},
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )
```

URL:

```python
path(
    "<int:post_id>/view/",
    FeedPostViewView.as_view(),
    name="feed-post-view",
),
```

---

# 18.30 — Avoid Duplicate Views

Do not create unlimited view rows from the same user.

A better production approach is to aggregate views.

For now, add:

```python
from django.utils import timezone
from datetime import timedelta
```

and only record one view per post per user per time period.

For example:

```python
recent_view = FeedInteraction.objects.filter(
    user=request.user,
    post_id=post_id,
    interaction_type=(
        FeedInteraction.InteractionType.VIEW
    ),
    created_at__gte=timezone.now()
    - timedelta(hours=24),
).exists()
```

Then:

```python
if not recent_view:
    FeedInteraction.objects.create(...)
```

---

# 18.31 — Feed Performance

As White Fiber grows, these database indexes become important.

For posts:

```python
class Meta:
    indexes = [
        models.Index(
            fields=[
                "author",
                "-created_at",
            ]
        ),
        models.Index(
            fields=[
                "visibility",
                "-created_at",
            ]
        ),
    ]
```

For reactions:

```python
models.Index(
    fields=[
        "post",
        "created_at",
    ]
)
```

For comments:

```python
models.Index(
    fields=[
        "post",
        "created_at",
    ]
)
```

Run:

```bash
python manage.py makemigrations
python manage.py migrate
```

---

# 18.32 — Cursor Pagination

For a large production feed, cursor pagination is preferable to:

```text
?page=1
?page=2
?page=3
```

because posts are constantly being inserted.

Configure:

`backend/config/pagination.py`

```python
from rest_framework.pagination import (
    CursorPagination,
)


class FeedCursorPagination(
    CursorPagination
):
    page_size = 20

    ordering = "-created_at"

    cursor_query_param = "cursor"
```

Then:

```text
GET /api/feed/?cursor=...
```

This gives a stable scrolling experience.

---

# 18.33 — Infinite Scroll

Your existing Intersection Observer can trigger the next request.

Flow:

```text
20 posts
   ↓
user scrolls
   ↓
sentinel visible
   ↓
GET /api/feed/?cursor=...
   ↓
append 20 posts
   ↓
repeat
```

Do **not** replace:

```javascript
setPosts(data.results)
```

with pagination blindly.

For subsequent pages use:

```javascript
setPosts(
  (previous) => [
    ...previous,
    ...data.results,
  ]
);
```

# 18.34 — Feed Tests

Create:

`backend/feed/tests.py`

```python
from django.test import TestCase

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model

from .ranking import (
    calculate_engagement_score,
    calculate_content_score,
)

User = get_user_model()


class FeedRankingTests(TestCase):

    def test_engagement_score(self):
        score = calculate_engagement_score(
            reaction_count=10,
            comment_count=5,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            score,
            35,
        )

    def test_media_score(self):
        self.assertEqual(
            calculate_content_score(
                has_media=True
            ),
            5,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            calculate_content_score(
                has_media=False
            ),
            0,
        )
```

Add API tests:

```text
✓ authenticated user receives feed
✓ anonymous user rejected
✓ blocked author excluded
✓ blocked-by-author excluded
✓ inactive author excluded
✓ suspended author excluded
✓ public posts visible
✓ friends-only posts visible to friends
✓ friends-only posts hidden from strangers
✓ private posts hidden
✓ own post visible
✓ engagement changes score
✓ recent posts receive recency score
✓ pagination works
✓ view tracking works
```

---

# 18.35 — Security Tests

Especially test this:

```text
User A blocks User B
        ↓
User B's post
        ↓
GET /api/feed/
        ↓
must NOT appear
```

Also:

```text
User B blocks User A
        ↓
User B's post
        ↓
User A feed
        ↓
must NOT appear
```

And:

```text
Private post
      ↓
non-friend
      ↓
GET /api/feed/
      ↓
404/not included
```

Never rely on:

```jsx
if (blocked) return null;
```

for security.

# 18.36 — Feed Ranking Configuration

Put tunable values in:

`backend/feed/constants.py`

```python
FRIEND_SCORE = 30
FOLLOWING_SCORE = 15
BASE_RELATIONSHIP_SCORE = 5

REACTION_WEIGHT = 2
COMMENT_WEIGHT = 3

MEDIA_SCORE = 5

RECENCY_MAX_SCORE = 30
RECENCY_HALF_LIFE_HOURS = 24

FEED_CANDIDATE_LIMIT = 200
FEED_PAGE_SIZE = 20
```

Then modify `ranking.py` to import them.

This means you can tune the feed without hunting through multiple files.

# 18.37 — Recommended Feed Rules

A practical initial White Fiber ranking system:

```text
                 POST
                  │
        ┌─────────┴─────────┐
        │                   │
    ELIGIBLE?             BLOCKED?
        │                   │
       YES                  NO
        │                   │
        └─────────┬─────────┘
                  ↓
              SCORE
                  │
       ┌──────────┼──────────┐
       ↓          ↓          ↓
   Relationship Engagement Recency
       │          │          │
       └──────────┼──────────┘
                  ↓
              Diversity
                  ↓
             Top 20 posts
```

**Next logical stage: Part 19 — Advanced Media System: image/video uploads, compression, thumbnails, multiple images per post, profile/cover photos, secure media URLs, storage optimization, and video-processing architecture.**

# 19.3 — Media model

### NEW FILE

`backend/media/models.py`

```python
from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models


class MediaAsset(models.Model):
    class MediaType(models.TextChoices):
        IMAGE = "image", "Image"
        VIDEO = "video", "Video"

    class ProcessingStatus(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = "pending", "Pending"
        PROCESSING = "processing", "Processing"
        READY = "ready", "Ready"
        FAILED = "failed", "Failed"

    owner = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="media_assets",
    )

    media_type = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=MediaType.choices,
    )

    file = models.FileField(
        upload_to="media/originals/%Y/%m/",
    )

    thumbnail = models.ImageField(
        upload_to="media/thumbnails/%Y/%m/",
        blank=True,
        null=True,
    )

    compressed_file = models.FileField(
        upload_to="media/processed/%Y/%m/",
        blank=True,
        null=True,
    )

    original_filename = models.CharField(
        max_length=255,
        blank=True,
    )

    mime_type = models.CharField(
        max_length=100,
        blank=True,
    )

    file_size = models.PositiveBigIntegerField(
        default=0,
    )

    width = models.PositiveIntegerField(
        null=True,
        blank=True,
    )

    height = models.PositiveIntegerField(
        null=True,
        blank=True,
    )

    duration_seconds = models.FloatField(
        null=True,
        blank=True,
    )

    processing_status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=ProcessingStatus.choices,
        default=ProcessingStatus.PENDING,
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(
        auto_now_add=True,
    )

    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(
        auto_now=True,
    )

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["created_at"]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["owner", "-created_at"]),
            models.Index(fields=["media_type", "processing_status"]),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.owner.username} - {self.media_type} - {self.id}"
```

# 19.5 — Media validation

### NEW FILE

`backend/media/validators.py`

```python
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError


MAX_IMAGE_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_VIDEO_SIZE = 100 * 1024 * 1024

ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES = {
    "image/jpeg",
    "image/png",
    "image/webp",
}

ALLOWED_VIDEO_TYPES = {
    "video/mp4",
    "video/webm",
    "video/quicktime",
}


def validate_media_file(file, media_type):
    if not file:
        raise ValidationError("Media file is required.")

    if media_type == "image":
        if file.size > MAX_IMAGE_SIZE:
            raise ValidationError(
                "Image size cannot exceed 5 MB."
            )

        if file.content_type not in ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES:
            raise ValidationError(
                "Unsupported image format."
            )

    elif media_type == "video":
        if file.size > MAX_VIDEO_SIZE:
            raise ValidationError(
                "Video size cannot exceed 100 MB."
            )

        if file.content_type not in ALLOWED_VIDEO_TYPES:
            raise ValidationError(
                "Unsupported video format."
            )

    else:
        raise ValidationError(
            "Unsupported media type."
        )
```

**Important security point:** `content_type` alone is not enough for production. Backend should also inspect the actual file signature/content before accepting uploads.

# 19.6 — Media serializer

### NEW FILE

`backend/media/serializers.py`

```python
from rest_framework import serializers

from .models import MediaAsset


class MediaAssetSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    thumbnail_url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = MediaAsset
        fields = [
            "id",
            "media_type",
            "url",
            "thumbnail_url",
            "original_filename",
            "mime_type",
            "file_size",
            "width",
            "height",
            "duration_seconds",
            "processing_status",
            "created_at",
        ]

        read_only_fields = fields

    def get_url(self, obj):
        request = self.context.get("request")

        if not obj.file:
            return None

        url = obj.file.url

        if request:
            return request.build_absolute_uri(url)

        return url

    def get_thumbnail_url(self, obj):
        request = self.context.get("request")

        if not obj.thumbnail:
            return None

        url = obj.thumbnail.url

        if request:
            return request.build_absolute_uri(url)

        return url
```

---

# 19.7 — Media upload service

### NEW FILE

`backend/media/services.py`

```python
from django.core.files.images import get_image_dimensions

from .models import MediaAsset
from .validators import validate_media_file


def create_media_asset(
    *,
    user,
    uploaded_file,
    media_type,
):
    validate_media_file(
        uploaded_file,
        media_type,
    )

    asset = MediaAsset.objects.create(
        owner=user,
        media_type=media_type,
        file=uploaded_file,
        original_filename=uploaded_file.name,
        mime_type=getattr(
            uploaded_file,
            "content_type",
            "",
        ),
        file_size=uploaded_file.size,
    )

    if media_type == MediaAsset.MediaType.IMAGE:
        try:
            width, height = get_image_dimensions(
                uploaded_file
            )

            asset.width = width
            asset.height = height
            asset.processing_status = (
                MediaAsset.ProcessingStatus.READY
            )
            asset.save(
                update_fields=[
                    "width",
                    "height",
                    "processing_status",
                    "updated_at",
                ]
            )
        except Exception:
            asset.processing_status = (
                MediaAsset.ProcessingStatus.FAILED
            )
            asset.save(
                update_fields=[
                    "processing_status",
                    "updated_at",
                ]
            )

    return asset
```

---

# 19.8 — Upload API

### NEW FILE

`backend/media/views.py`

```python
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.parsers import MultiPartParser, FormParser
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from .serializers import MediaAssetSerializer
from .services import create_media_asset


class MediaUploadView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    parser_classes = [
        MultiPartParser,
        FormParser,
    ]

    def post(self, request):
        uploaded_file = request.FILES.get("file")
        media_type = request.data.get("media_type")

        if not uploaded_file:
            return Response(
                {"detail": "File is required."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        if media_type not in {"image", "video"}:
            return Response(
                {"detail": "Invalid media type."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        asset = create_media_asset(
            user=request.user,
            uploaded_file=uploaded_file,
            media_type=media_type,
        )

        return Response(
            MediaAssetSerializer(
                asset,
                context={"request": request},
            ).data,
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )
```

### NEW FILE

```javascript
import api from "./api";

export async function uploadMedia(file, mediaType, onUploadProgress) {
  const formData = new FormData();

  formData.append("file", file);
  formData.append("media_type", mediaType);

  const response = await api.post(
    "/media/upload/",
    formData,
    {
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "multipart/form-data",
      },
      onUploadProgress,
    }
  );

  return response.data;
}
```

# 19.11 — Media utility

### NEW FILE

`frontend/src/utils/media.js`

```javascript
export function getMediaType(file) {
  if (file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
    return "image";
  }

  if (file.type.startsWith("video/")) {
    return "video";
  }

  return null;
}

export function formatFileSize(bytes) {
  if (!bytes) return "0 B";

  const units = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB"];

  let size = bytes;
  let index = 0;

  while (size >= 1024 && index < units.length - 1) {
    size /= 1024;
    index += 1;
  }

  return `${size.toFixed(1)} ${units[index]}`;
}
```

---

# 19.12 — MediaUploader component

### NEW FILE

`frontend/src/components/media/MediaUploader.jsx`

```jsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { uploadMedia } from "../../services/mediaService";
import { getMediaType } from "../../utils/media";

export default function MediaUploader({ onUploaded }) {
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  async function handleChange(event) {
    const files = Array.from(event.target.files || []);

    if (!files.length) {
      return;
    }

    setError("");
    setUploading(true);
    setProgress(0);

    try {
      const uploaded = [];

      for (const file of files) {
        const mediaType = getMediaType(file);

        if (!mediaType) {
          throw new Error(
            `Unsupported file: ${file.name}`
          );
        }

        const result = await uploadMedia(
          file,
          mediaType,
          (event) => {
            if (!event.total) return;

            setProgress(
              Math.round(
                (event.loaded / event.total) * 100
              )
            );
          }
        );

        uploaded.push(result);
      }

      onUploaded?.(uploaded);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.detail ||
        err.message ||
        "Upload failed."
      );
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }

    event.target.value = "";
  }

  return (
    <div className="media-uploader">
      <label>
        Add photos/videos

        <input
          type="file"
          accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,video/mp4,video/webm,video/quicktime"
          multiple
          onChange={handleChange}
          disabled={uploading}
        />
      </label>

      {uploading && (
        <div className="upload-progress">
          Uploading {progress}%
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <p className="upload-error">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

# 19.13 — Media grid

```jsx
import VideoPlayer from "./VideoPlayer";

export default function MediaGrid({ media = [] }) {
  if (!media.length) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div className={`media-grid media-count-${Math.min(media.length, 4)}`}>
      {media.map((item) => {
        if (item.media_type === "video") {
          return (
            <VideoPlayer
              key={item.id}
              src={item.url}
              poster={item.thumbnail_url}
            />
          );
        }

        return (
          <img
            key={item.id}
            src={item.url}
            alt={item.original_filename || "Post media"}
            loading="lazy"
          />
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}
```

# 19.17 — Secure ownership check

User kisi aur user ki media ko apne post mein attach nahi kar sakta.

### MODIFY EXISTING

Post creation logic mein:

```python
from media.models import MediaAsset


def attach_media_to_post(post, user, media_ids):
    if not media_ids:
        return

    media_assets = MediaAsset.objects.filter(
        id__in=media_ids,
        owner=user,
    )

    if media_assets.count() != len(set(media_ids)):
        raise ValueError(
            "One or more media assets are invalid."
        )

    post.media_assets.set(media_assets)
```

Iska main point:

```text
owner=user
```

hona mandatory hai.

# 19.18 — Image compression

Browser side compression ke liye package:

```bash
cd white-fiber/frontend
npm install browser-image-compression
```

### MODIFY EXISTING

`frontend/src/services/mediaService.js`

```javascript
import imageCompression from "browser-image-compression";
import api from "./api";

export async function prepareImage(file) {
  if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
    return file;
  }

  return imageCompression(file, {
    maxSizeMB: 2,
    maxWidthOrHeight: 2048,
    useWebWorker: true,
  });
}

export async function uploadMedia(
  file,
  mediaType,
  onUploadProgress
) {
  const processedFile =
    mediaType === "image"
      ? await prepareImage(file)
      : file;

  const formData = new FormData();

  formData.append(
    "file",
    processedFile,
    processedFile.name
  );

  formData.append(
    "media_type",
    mediaType
  );

  const response = await api.post(
    "/media/upload/",
    formData,
    {
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "multipart/form-data",
      },
      onUploadProgress,
    }
  );

  return response.data;
}
```

Isse mobile users ka upload bandwidth bhi kam hoga.

# 19.19 — Thumbnail generation

Images ke liye backend thumbnail generate kar sakta hai.

Install:

```bash
cd white-fiber/backend
pip install Pillow
```

### NEW FILE

`backend/media/image_processing.py`

```python
from io import BytesIO

from django.core.files.base import ContentFile
from PIL import Image


def generate_thumbnail(asset):
    if asset.media_type != "image":
        return

    if not asset.file:
        return

    image = Image.open(asset.file)
    image = image.convert("RGB")

    image.thumbnail((800, 800))

    output = BytesIO()

    image.save(
        output,
        format="JPEG",
        quality=82,
        optimize=True,
    )

    filename = (
        f"{asset.id}_thumbnail.jpg"
    )

    asset.thumbnail.save(
        filename,
        ContentFile(output.getvalue()),
        save=False,
    )

    asset.save(
        update_fields=[
            "thumbnail",
            "updated_at",
        ]
    )
```

Then service mein:

```python
from .image_processing import generate_thumbnail
```

aur image processing ke baad:

```python
generate_thumbnail(asset)
```

# 19.20 — Video processing architecture

Video ko Django request ke andar process **mat** karo.

Production architecture:

```text
React
  │
  │ Upload
  ▼
Django
  │
  ├── Save original
  │
  └── Queue processing job
          │
          ▼
       Redis
          │
          ▼
   Background Worker
          │
          ├── Validate video
          ├── Generate thumbnail
          ├── Compress/transcode
          └── Extract metadata
          │
          ▼
      Object Storage
```

Isse 100 MB video process karte waqt HTTP request hang nahi hogi.

# 19.22 — Video task

### NEW FILE

`backend/media/tasks.py`

```python
from celery import shared_task

from .models import MediaAsset


@shared_task
def process_video(media_id):
    try:
        asset = MediaAsset.objects.get(
            id=media_id,
        )
    except MediaAsset.DoesNotExist:
        return

    asset.processing_status = (
        MediaAsset.ProcessingStatus.PROCESSING
    )
    asset.save(
        update_fields=[
            "processing_status",
            "updated_at",
        ]
    )

    try:
        # Production implementation:
        #
        # 1. Validate video
        # 2. Extract duration
        # 3. Extract dimensions
        # 4. Generate thumbnail
        # 5. Transcode/compress
        # 6. Save processed file
        #
        # FFmpeg integration goes here.

        asset.processing_status = (
            MediaAsset.ProcessingStatus.READY
        )

        asset.save(
            update_fields=[
                "processing_status",
                "updated_at",
            ]
        )

    except Exception:
        asset.processing_status = (
            MediaAsset.ProcessingStatus.FAILED
        )

        asset.save(
            update_fields=[
                "processing_status",
                "updated_at",
            ]
        )
```

Actual FFmpeg processing ko next media-processing layer mein isolate karna better hai, instead of putting huge processing logic inside the API view.

# 19.23 — Queue video processing

### MODIFY EXISTING

`backend/media/services.py`

Import:

```python
from .tasks import process_video
```

Video asset create hone ke baad:

```python
if media_type == MediaAsset.MediaType.VIDEO:
    process_video.delay(asset.id)
```

So upload request:

```text
Upload
 ↓
Django saves file
 ↓
HTTP response
 ↓
Celery processes video
```

instead of:

```text
Upload
 ↓
Django waits 30–120 seconds
 ↓
Process video
 ↓
Response
```

```jsx
{item.processing_status === "pending" && (

{item.processing_status === "ready" && (

    src={item.url}
```

### NEW FILE

```javascript
import { Capacitor } from "@capacitor/core";
import {
  Camera,
  CameraResultType,
  CameraSource,
} from "@capacitor/camera";

export async function pickPhoto() {
  if (!Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) {
    return null;
  }

  const result = await Camera.getPhoto({
    source: CameraSource.Prompt,
    resultType: CameraResultType.Uri,
    quality: 85,

  return result;
}
```

This gives Android users native camera/gallery access.

# 19.26 — Production storage

Development:

```env
MEDIA_ROOT=/app/media
```

Production should preferably use object storage:

```text
React
   │
   ▼
Django API
   │
   ▼
Object Storage
   ├── originals/
   ├── thumbnails/
   └── processed/
```

Example storage layout:

```text
media/
├── originals/
│   ├── 2026/
│   │   └── 09/
│
├── thumbnails/
│   ├── 2026/
│   │   └── 09/
│
└── processed/
    ├── 2026/
    │   └── 09/
```

Do **not** store thousands/millions of production images/videos inside the application container filesystem.

---

# 19.27 — Media security

Backend must enforce:

```text
✓ authentication
✓ file size
✓ actual file type
✓ allowed extensions
✓ MIME validation
✓ ownership
✓ upload rate limiting
✓ malware scanning where appropriate
✓ processing limits
✓ video duration limits
✓ image dimension limits
✓ blocked/suspended users
```

For private media, don't expose unrestricted permanent URLs.

Use authorization/signed URLs or an authenticated media endpoint.

---

# 19.28 — Video limits

Recommended application-level limits:

```python
MAX_VIDEO_SIZE = 100 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_VIDEO_DURATION = 180
MAX_IMAGE_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_IMAGE_DIMENSION = 8192
```

For example, a 10-minute 4K video should not enter the same processing queue as a short normal post video.

---

# 19.29 — Nginx upload limit

### MODIFY EXISTING

Production Nginx server:

```nginx
server {
    # existing configuration...

    client_max_body_size 100M;

    location /api/ {
        proxy_pass http://backend:8000;

        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }
}
```

This must match your actual backend upload limits.

# 20.3 — Device model verify karo

Part 13.2 mein humne `UserDevice` model banaya tha.

### EXISTING FILE

`backend/notifications/models.py`

Ensure:

```python
class UserDevice(models.Model):
    PLATFORM_CHOICES = (
        ("android", "Android"),
        ("ios", "iOS"),
        ("web", "Web"),
    )

    user = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="devices",
    )

    token = models.TextField(unique=True)

    platform = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=PLATFORM_CHOICES,
        default="android",
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(
        auto_now_add=True
    )

    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(
        auto_now=True
    )

    def __str__(self):
        return (
            f"{self.user.username} - "
            f"{self.platform}"
        )
```

Agar already same model hai to **dobara create mat karna**.

# 20.6 — Firebase initialization

### NEW FILE

`backend/notifications/firebase.py`

```python
import os

import firebase_admin
from firebase_admin import credentials


def initialize_firebase():
    if firebase_admin._apps:
        return

    credentials_path = os.getenv(
        "FIREBASE_CREDENTIALS_PATH"
    )

    if not credentials_path:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "FIREBASE_CREDENTIALS_PATH is not configured."
        )

    cred = credentials.Certificate(
        credentials_path
    )

    firebase_admin.initialize_app(cred)
```

---

Production Docker/container mein actual secure path use karna.

**Firebase service-account private key ko frontend mein kabhi nahi rakhna.**

# 20.8 — Push notification service

### NEW FILE

`backend/notifications/push_service.py`

```python
from firebase_admin import messaging

from .firebase import initialize_firebase
from .models import UserDevice


def send_push_notification(
    *,
    user,
    title,
    body,
    data=None,
):
    initialize_firebase()

    devices = UserDevice.objects.filter(
        user=user,
        platform="android",
    )

    if not devices.exists():
        return

    data = data or {}

    for device in devices:
        message = messaging.Message(
            notification=messaging.Notification(
                title=title,
                body=body,
            ),
            data={
                str(key): str(value)
                for key, value in data.items()
            },
            token=device.token,
        )

        try:
            messaging.send(message)
        except Exception:
            # Invalid/stale tokens should eventually
            # be removed or marked inactive.
            continue
```

Ab Django kisi user ko Android push bhej sakta hai.

```python
Notification.objects.create(

    post_id=post.id,
```

Uske baad:

```python

    user=post.author,
    title="New comment",
    body="Someone commented on your post.",
    data={
        "type": "comment",
        "post_id": post.id,

)
```

Isliye centralized service banayenge.

# 20.10 — Central Notification Service

### NEW FILE

`backend/notifications/services.py`

```python
from .models import Notification
from .push_service import send_push_notification


def create_notification(
    *,
    recipient,
    sender=None,
    notification_type,
    message,
    post_id=None,
    comment_id=None,
    conversation_id=None,
    push_title=None,
    push_body=None,
    push_data=None,
):
    notification = Notification.objects.create(
        recipient=recipient,
        sender=sender,
        notification_type=notification_type,
        message=message,
        post_id=post_id,
        comment_id=comment_id,
        conversation_id=conversation_id,
    )

    if push_title and push_body:
        send_push_notification(
            user=recipient,
            title=push_title,
            body=push_body,
            data=push_data or {},
        )

    return notification
```

# 20.11 — Example: Friend request

### MODIFY EXISTING

Jahan friend request notification create hoti hai:

Old:

```python
Notification.objects.create(
    recipient=receiver,
    sender=request.user,
    notification_type=Notification.NotificationType.FRIEND_REQUEST,
    message="You received a friend request.",
)
```

New:

```python
from notifications.services import create_notification


create_notification(
    recipient=receiver,
    sender=request.user,
    notification_type=(
        Notification.NotificationType.FRIEND_REQUEST
    ),
    message="You received a friend request.",
    push_title="New friend request",
    push_body=(
        f"{request.user.username} sent you a friend request."
    ),
    push_data={
        "type": "friend_request",
        "user_id": request.user.id,
    },
)
```

Existing database notification bhi create hogi aur Android push bhi.

# 20.15 — Don't send push when user is actively chatting

Agar user already same conversation mein hai, unnecessary push notification avoid karna better hai.

Basic logic:

```text
User A sends message
       │
       ▼
Is User B currently viewing
this conversation?
       │
    ┌──┴──┐
   YES    NO
    │      │
 no push  push
```

Iske liye later WebSocket presence/active conversation state maintain kar sakte hain.

At minimum, backend push service mein future parameter rakho:

```python
send_push_notification(
    user=recipient,
    title="New message",
    body="...",
    data={
        "type": "message",
        "conversation_id": conversation.id,
    },
)
```

Frontend active conversation ke basis par duplicate notification suppress kar sakta hai.

# 20.17 — Capacitor push initialization

Part 13.2 ka existing code preserve karo.

### EXISTING/MODIFY

`frontend/src/services/pushService.js`

```javascript
import { PushNotifications } from "@capacitor/push-notifications";

export async function initializePushNotifications() {
  try {
    let permission =
      await PushNotifications.checkPermissions();

    if (permission.receive === "prompt") {
      permission =
        await PushNotifications.requestPermissions();
    }

    if (permission.receive !== "granted") {
      return false;
    }

    await PushNotifications.register();

    return true;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Push notification initialization failed:",
      error
    );

    return false;
  }
}
```

---

# 20.18 — Register token with Django

### MODIFY EXISTING

`pushService.js`

```javascript
import { PushNotifications } from "@capacitor/push-notifications";
import api from "./api";


export async function initializePushNotifications() {
  try {
    let permission =
      await PushNotifications.checkPermissions();

    if (permission.receive === "prompt") {
      permission =
        await PushNotifications.requestPermissions();
    }

    if (permission.receive !== "granted") {
      return false;
    }

    await PushNotifications.register();

    return true;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Push notification initialization failed:",
      error
    );

    return false;
  }
}


export function registerPushListeners() {
  PushNotifications.addListener(
    "registration",
    async (token) => {
      try {
        await api.post(
          "/notifications/devices/",
          {
            token: token.value,
            platform: "android",
          }
        );
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(
          "Failed to register push token:",
          error
        );
      }
    }
  );

  PushNotifications.addListener(
    "registrationError",
    (error) => {
      console.error(
        "Push registration error:",
        error
      );
    }
  );
}
```

# 20.22 — Foreground notification

### MODIFY

`frontend/src/services/pushService.js`

```javascript
export function registerForegroundListener(
  onNotification
) {
  return PushNotifications.addListener(
    "pushNotificationReceived",
    (notification) => {
      onNotification?.(notification);
    }
  );
}
```

App active ho to custom in-app notification/toast dikha sakte ho.

# 20.23 — Notification tap → correct page

### NEW FILE

`frontend/src/utils/notificationNavigation.js`

```javascript
export function getNotificationPath(
  notification
) {
  const data = notification?.data || {};

  switch (data.type) {
    case "friend_request":
      return "/friend-requests";

    case "reaction":
    case "comment":
      return data.post_id
        ? `/posts/${data.post_id}`
        : "/notifications";

    case "message":
      return data.conversation_id
        ? `/messages/${data.conversation_id}`
        : "/messages";

    case "friend_accepted":
      return "/friends";

    default:
      return "/notifications";
  }
}
```

```javascript
import { App } from "@capacitor/app";
```

```javascript
export function registerNotificationActions(
  navigate
) {
  PushNotifications.addListener(
    "pushNotificationActionPerformed",
    (action) => {
      const path =
        getNotificationPath(
          action.notification
        );

      navigate(path);
    }
  );
}
```

```text

      ↓
User taps
      ↓

      ↓
Relevant page
```

# 20.30 — Push notification duplication control

Ek important issue:

```text
Comment
 ↓
Notification DB
 ↓
Push
```

Agar same event do baar trigger hua to user ko duplicate push mil sakti hai.

Isliye service layer mein future idempotency support rakho.

Simple protection:

```python
Notification.objects.filter(
    recipient=recipient,
    sender=sender,
    notification_type=notification_type,
    post_id=post_id,
    comment_id=comment_id,
).exists()
```

Lekin har notification type ke liye same deduplication logic appropriate nahi hota. Message notifications ko normally deduplicate karne ke bajaye message-specific IDs/data use karna better hai.

---

# 20.31 — Push rate protection

Agar kisi post par 1,000 reactions hain:

```text
1,000 reactions
      ↓
1,000 pushes
```

bad UX hai.

Future batching/grouping:

```text
"25 people reacted to your post"
```

Better hai.

Part 20 mein notification service ko centralized karne se future batching possible ho jati hai.

---

# 20.32 — Firebase token cleanup

Firebase agar invalid token return kare to stale device remove karna chahiye.

Service ko later improve karenge:

```python
from firebase_admin import messaging


try:
    messaging.send(message)

except messaging.UnregisteredError:
    device.delete()

except messaging.SenderIdMismatchError:
    device.delete()
```

Baaki unexpected errors ko blindly device delete nahi karna.

Admin dashboard mein optionally:

```text

-----------------------

```

add kiye ja sakte hain.

### NEW MODEL

`backend/notifications/models.py`

```python
class PushDeliveryLog(models.Model):
    user = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="push_delivery_logs",
    )

    notification = models.ForeignKey(
        "notifications.Notification",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="push_delivery_logs",
    )

    device = models.ForeignKey(
        "notifications.UserDevice",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="push_delivery_logs",
    )

    success = models.BooleanField(
        default=False
    )

    error_message = models.TextField(
        blank=True
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(
        auto_now_add=True
    )
```

**Note:** High-volume production app mein is log table ko indefinitely grow nahi karna; retention/cleanup policy honi chahiye.

# 20.34 — Background push architecture

Final system:

```text
User A
  │
  │ Comment
  ▼
Django API
  │
  ├── Create Notification
  │
  └── Queue Push
          │
          ▼
        Redis
          │
          ▼
       Worker
          │
          ▼
       Firebase
          │
          ▼
    Android Device
```

Production mein push sending ko request-response cycle se separate karna better hai.

Isliye Celery task:

### MODIFY/NEW

`backend/notifications/tasks.py`

```python
from celery import shared_task

from .models import UserDevice
from .push_service import send_push_notification


@shared_task
def send_notification_push(
    user_id,
    title,
    body,
    data=None,
):
    from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model

    User = get_user_model()

    try:
        user = User.objects.get(
            id=user_id
        )
    except User.DoesNotExist:
        return

    send_push_notification(
        user=user,
        title=title,
        body=body,
        data=data or {},
    )
```

Then `services.py` mein:

```python
from .tasks import send_notification_push
```

Instead of directly:

```python
send_push_notification(...)
```

use:

```python
send_notification_push.delay(
    recipient.id,
    push_title,
    push_body,
    push_data or {},
)
```

Isse Django API ko Firebase response ka wait nahi karna padega.

# 20.35 — Notification service final version

Central service ka important part:

```python
notification = Notification.objects.create(
    recipient=recipient,
    sender=sender,
    notification_type=notification_type,
    message=message,
    post_id=post_id,
    comment_id=comment_id,
    conversation_id=conversation_id,
)

if push_title and push_body:
    send_notification_push.delay(
        recipient.id,
        push_title,
        push_body,
        push_data or {},
    )
```

Yeh architecture Part 20 ka main improvement hai.

# 21.3 — Conversation model extend karo

### MODIFY EXISTING

`backend/messaging/models.py`

Existing fields ke saath add:

```python id="c1x8sv"
class Conversation(models.Model):
    class ConversationType(models.TextChoices):
        DIRECT = "direct", "Direct"
        GROUP = "group", "Group"

    participants = models.ManyToManyField(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        related_name="conversations",
    )

    conversation_type = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=ConversationType.choices,
        default=ConversationType.DIRECT,
    )

    name = models.CharField(
        max_length=100,
        blank=True,
    )

    avatar = models.ImageField(
        upload_to="conversations/",
        blank=True,
        null=True,
    )

    created_by = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="created_conversations",
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(
        auto_now_add=True,
    )

    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(
        auto_now=True,
    )
```

Existing `Meta` aur `__str__` ko preserve/adapt karo.

# 21.4 — Conversation membership model

Group chat ke liye simple ManyToMany sufficient nahi hai.

Humein membership information bhi chahiye.

### NEW MODEL

`backend/messaging/models.py`

```python id="b3gq1e"
class ConversationMember(models.Model):
    class Role(models.TextChoices):
        MEMBER = "member", "Member"
        ADMIN = "admin", "Admin"

    conversation = models.ForeignKey(
        Conversation,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="memberships",
    )

    user = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="conversation_memberships",
    )

    role = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=Role.choices,
        default=Role.MEMBER,
    )

    is_muted = models.BooleanField(
        default=False,
    )

    joined_at = models.DateTimeField(
        auto_now_add=True,
    )

    last_read_at = models.DateTimeField(
        null=True,
        blank=True,
    )

    class Meta:
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=[
                    "conversation",
                    "user",
                ],
                name="unique_conversation_member",
            ),
        ]

        indexes = [
            models.Index(
                fields=[
                    "user",
                    "conversation",
                ]
            ),
        ]
```

Ab group member ke paas role bhi ho sakta hai:

```text
ADMIN
MEMBER
```

# 21.7 — Message content validation

Existing:

```python
content = models.TextField()
```

ko preserve karo.

Serializer validation:

```python id="r7n3sd"
def validate_content(self, value):
    value = value.strip()

    if len(value) > 5000:
        raise serializers.ValidationError(
            "Message is too long."
        )

    return value
```

Empty message allow nahi karna **unless attachment present ho**.

# 21.8 — Message serializer

### MODIFY EXISTING

`backend/messaging/serializers.py`

```python id="s7p4cg"
class MessageSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    sender = MessageUserSerializer(
        read_only=True
    )

    reply_to = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = Message

        fields = [
            "id",
            "conversation",
            "sender",
            "content",
            "attachment",
            "is_read",
            "is_deleted",
            "edited_at",
            "reply_to",
            "created_at",
            "updated_at",
        ]

        read_only_fields = [
            "id",
            "sender",
            "is_read",
            "is_deleted",
            "edited_at",
            "created_at",
            "updated_at",
        ]

    def get_reply_to(self, obj):
        if not obj.reply_to:
            return None

        return {
            "id": obj.reply_to.id,
            "content": (
                "[deleted]"
                if obj.reply_to.is_deleted
                else obj.reply_to.content
            ),
            "sender_id": obj.reply_to.sender_id,
        }
```

---

# 21.9 — Message edit API

### NEW VIEW

`backend/messaging/views.py`

```python id="v8x0sk"
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from .models import Message


class EditMessageView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def patch(self, request, message_id):
        try:
            message = Message.objects.get(
                id=message_id,
                sender=request.user,
            )
        except Message.DoesNotExist:
            return Response(
                {"detail": "Message not found."},
                status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            )

        if message.is_deleted:
            return Response(
                {"detail": "Deleted messages cannot be edited."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        content = request.data.get(
            "content",
            "",
        ).strip()

        if not content:
            return Response(
                {"detail": "Message cannot be empty."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        if len(content) > 5000:
            return Response(
                {"detail": "Message is too long."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        message.content = content
        message.edited_at = timezone.now()

        message.save(
            update_fields=[
                "content",
                "edited_at",
                "updated_at",
            ]
        )

        return Response(
            MessageSerializer(message).data
        )
```

---

# 21.10 — Delete message API

### NEW VIEW

```python id="0o5j2q"
class DeleteMessageView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def delete(self, request, message_id):
        try:
            message = Message.objects.get(
                id=message_id,
                sender=request.user,
            )
        except Message.DoesNotExist:
            return Response(
                {"detail": "Message not found."},
                status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            )

        message.content = ""
        message.attachment = None
        message.is_deleted = True

        message.save(
            update_fields=[
                "content",
                "attachment",
                "is_deleted",
                "updated_at",
            ]
        )

        return Response(
            {"success": True},
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK,
        )
```

# 21.12 — Permission: conversation membership

Yeh **bahut important security requirement** hai.

User ko sirf JWT mil jane se kisi bhi conversation ka data access nahi milna chahiye.

### NEW FILE

`backend/messaging/permissions.py`

```python id="q2k8yv"
from .models import ConversationMember


def is_conversation_member(
    user,
    conversation_id,
):
    return ConversationMember.objects.filter(
        conversation_id=conversation_id,
        user=user,
    ).exists()
```

# 21.14 — WebSocket security

```python id="h1s8qa"
    self.user,

```

Yeh check **JWT authentication ke baad** hona chahiye.

Final:

```text
JWT valid?
   │
   ├── NO → close
   │
   └── YES
        │
        ▼
Conversation member?
        │
     ┌──┴──┐
    NO    YES
    │       │
 close    connect
```

# 21.15 — Block protection

```python id="j0u4xk"
from moderation.models import UserBlock


def users_blocked(user_a, user_b):
    return UserBlock.objects.filter(
        blocker=user_a,
        blocked=user_b,
    ).exists() or UserBlock.objects.filter(
        blocker=user_b,
        blocked=user_a,
    ).exists()
```

Agar block hai:

```text
Messaging
   ↓
403 / access denied
```

# 21.16 — Group conversation creation

### NEW VIEW

Concept:

```python id="j7m6cc"
class CreateGroupConversationView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request):
        name = request.data.get("name", "").strip()
        user_ids = request.data.get("user_ids", [])

        if not name:
            return Response(
                {"detail": "Group name is required."},
                status=400,
            )

        if not user_ids:
            return Response(
                {"detail": "Group members are required."},
                status=400,
            )

        conversation = Conversation.objects.create(
            conversation_type=(
                Conversation.ConversationType.GROUP
            ),
            name=name,
            created_by=request.user,
        )

        conversation.participants.add(
            request.user,
            *user_ids,
        )

        ConversationMember.objects.create(
            conversation=conversation,
            user=request.user,
            role=ConversationMember.Role.ADMIN,
        )

        for user_id in user_ids:
            ConversationMember.objects.get_or_create(
                conversation=conversation,
                user_id=user_id,
                defaults={
                    "role": ConversationMember.Role.MEMBER,
                },
            )

        return Response(
            {
                "id": conversation.id,
                "name": conversation.name,
                "type": conversation.conversation_type,
            },
            status=201,
        )
```

Production mein `user_ids` validate karna zaroori hai:

* inactive users
* blocked users
* suspended users
* nonexistent users

ko add nahi karna.

# 21.21 — Frontend typing hook

### NEW FILE

`frontend/src/hooks/useTypingIndicator.js`

```javascript id="7h4m2n"
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";

export default function useTypingIndicator(
  socket,
  value
) {
  const timeoutRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!socket || socket.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
      return;
    }

    if (!value.trim()) {
      socket.send(
        JSON.stringify({
          type: "typing",
          is_typing: false,
        })
      );

      return;
    }

    socket.send(
      JSON.stringify({
        type: "typing",
        is_typing: true,
      })
    );

    clearTimeout(timeoutRef.current);

    timeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
      if (socket.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
        socket.send(
          JSON.stringify({
            type: "typing",
            is_typing: false,
          })
        );
      }
    }, 1200);

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timeoutRef.current);
    };
  }, [socket, value]);
}
```

```jsx id="6efj85"
      onClick={() => setReplyingTo(null)}
    >
      Cancel
    </button>
  </div>
)}
```

```jsx id="9h7v2f"
export default function MessageActions({
  message,
  onReply,
  onEdit,
  onDelete,
}) {
  const ownMessage =
    message.sender?.id ===
    message.currentUserId;

  return (
    <div className="message-actions">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => onReply(message)}
      >
        Reply
      </button>

      {ownMessage && !message.is_deleted && (
        <>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onEdit(message)}
          >
            Edit
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onDelete(message)}
          >
            Delete
          </button>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

# 21.31 — Better read tracking

Current:

```python
is_read = BooleanField()
```

Direct messages ke liye okay hai.

Group chats ke liye problematic hai because:

```text
Message
  ├── User A read
  ├── User B read
  ├── User C unread
```

One boolean can't represent this.

Isliye `ConversationMember.last_read_at` use karenge.

Unread query:

```python id="q9a4u3"
Message.objects.filter(
    conversation=conversation,
    created_at__gt=membership.last_read_at,
).exclude(
    sender=request.user,
).count()
```

Part 21 mein group chat ke liye **member-level read state** preferred hai.

# 5. Story Model

## NEW FILE — `backend/stories/models.py`

```python
from datetime import timedelta

from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models
from django.utils import timezone


class Story(models.Model):
    class Visibility(models.TextChoices):
        PUBLIC = "public", "Public"
        FRIENDS = "friends", "Friends"

    owner = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="stories",
    )

    media = models.ForeignKey(
        "media.MediaAsset",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="stories",
    )

    visibility = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=Visibility.choices,
        default=Visibility.FRIENDS,
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(
        auto_now_add=True,
    )

    expires_at = models.DateTimeField()

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["created_at"]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["owner", "-created_at"]),
            models.Index(fields=["expires_at"]),
            models.Index(fields=["visibility", "expires_at"]),
        ]

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.expires_at:
            self.expires_at = timezone.now() + timedelta(hours=24)

        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    @property
    def is_expired(self):
        return timezone.now() >= self.expires_at

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.owner.username} Story #{self.id}"
```

### Important

We are intentionally using:

```python
"media.MediaAsset"
```

rather than creating another image/video model.

That keeps Part 19 and Part 22 connected.

# 6. Story View Model

We need to know which Stories a user has already seen.

## MODIFY EXISTING FILE — `backend/stories/models.py`

Add below `Story`:

```python
class StoryView(models.Model):
    story = models.ForeignKey(
        Story,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="views",
    )

    viewer = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="story_views",
    )

    viewed_at = models.DateTimeField(
        auto_now_add=True,
    )

    class Meta:
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["story", "viewer"],
                name="unique_story_viewer",
            ),
        ]

        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["viewer", "-viewed_at"]),
            models.Index(fields=["story", "-viewed_at"]),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return (
            f"{self.viewer.username} viewed "
            f"Story #{self.story.id}"
        )
```

A user viewing the same Story multiple times will still have only one `StoryView` record.

---

# 7. Story Permissions

Stories must respect your existing:

* Friends
* Blocks
* Suspensions
* Account status

## NEW FILE — `backend/stories/permissions.py`

```python
from django.db.models import Q

from friends.models import Friendship
from moderation.models import UserBlock


def get_friend_ids(user):
    friendships = Friendship.objects.filter(
        Q(user1=user) | Q(user2=user)
    ).values_list(
        "user1_id",
        "user2_id",
    )

    friend_ids = set()

    for user_one_id, user_two_id in friendships:
        if user_one_id != user.id:
            friend_ids.add(user_one_id)

        if user_two_id != user.id:
            friend_ids.add(user_two_id)

    return friend_ids


def get_blocked_user_ids(user):
    blocked_by_me = UserBlock.objects.filter(
        blocker=user
    ).values_list(
        "blocked_id",
        flat=True,
    )

    blocked_me = UserBlock.objects.filter(
        blocked=user
    ).values_list(
        "blocker_id",
        flat=True,
    )

    return set(blocked_by_me).union(blocked_me)


def can_view_story(user, story):
    if story.owner_id == user.id:
        return True

    if not story.owner.is_active:
        return False

    if getattr(story.owner, "is_suspended", False):
        return False

    blocked_ids = get_blocked_user_ids(user)

    if story.owner_id in blocked_ids:
        return False

    if story.visibility == "public":
        return True

    friend_ids = get_friend_ids(user)

    return story.owner_id in friend_ids
```

# 8. Story Services

## NEW FILE — `backend/stories/services.py`

```python
from django.utils import timezone

from .models import Story, StoryView


def get_active_stories():
    return Story.objects.filter(
        expires_at__gt=timezone.now()
    )


def mark_story_viewed(user, story):
    if story.owner_id == user.id:
        return None

    view, _ = StoryView.objects.get_or_create(
        story=story,
        viewer=user,
    )

    return view


def delete_story(story):
    story.delete()
```

# 9. Story Serializers

## NEW FILE — `backend/stories/serializers.py`

```python
from rest_framework import serializers

from .models import Story, StoryView


class StoryOwnerSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    id = serializers.IntegerField()
    username = serializers.CharField()
    first_name = serializers.CharField()
    last_name = serializers.CharField()
    avatar = serializers.CharField(
        allow_null=True,
        required=False,
    )


class StorySerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    owner = StoryOwnerSerializer(
        read_only=True
    )

    media_url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    thumbnail_url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    media_type = serializers.CharField(
        source="media.media_type",
        read_only=True,
    )

    is_seen = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = Story

        fields = [
            "id",
            "owner",
            "media_url",
            "thumbnail_url",
            "media_type",
            "visibility",
            "created_at",
            "expires_at",
            "is_seen",
        ]

        read_only_fields = [
            "id",
            "owner",
            "media_url",
            "thumbnail_url",
            "media_type",
            "created_at",
            "expires_at",
            "is_seen",
        ]

    def get_media_url(self, obj):
        if not obj.media or not obj.media.file:
            return None

        request = self.context.get("request")

        url = obj.media.file.url

        if request:
            return request.build_absolute_uri(url)

        return url

    def get_thumbnail_url(self, obj):
        if not obj.media or not obj.media.thumbnail:
            return self.get_media_url(obj)

        request = self.context.get("request")

        url = obj.media.thumbnail.url

        if request:
            return request.build_absolute_uri(url)

        return url

    def get_is_seen(self, obj):
        user = self.context["request"].user

        return StoryView.objects.filter(
            story=obj,
            viewer=user,
        ).exists()
```

# 10. Story List Logic

We want the API to return Stories grouped by user.

Example:

```text
John
 ├── Story 1
 ├── Story 2
 └── Story 3

Sarah
 ├── Story 1
 └── Story 2
```

Unseen users should appear before users whose Stories have already been watched.

# 11. Story Views

## NEW FILE — `backend/stories/views.py`

```python
from django.db.models import Exists, OuterRef, Q
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404
from django.utils import timezone

from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.parsers import FormParser, MultiPartParser
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from media.models import MediaAsset

from .models import Story, StoryView
from .permissions import (
    can_view_story,
    get_blocked_user_ids,
    get_friend_ids,
)
from .serializers import StorySerializer


class StoryListView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        now = timezone.now()

        friend_ids = get_friend_ids(request.user)
        blocked_ids = get_blocked_user_ids(request.user)

        visible_filter = (
            Q(owner_id=request.user.id)
            | Q(
                visibility=Story.Visibility.PUBLIC,
                owner__is_active=True,
            )
            | Q(
                visibility=Story.Visibility.FRIENDS,
                owner_id__in=friend_ids,
            )
        )

        stories = (
            Story.objects
            .select_related(
                "owner",
                "media",
            )
            .filter(
                visible_filter,
                expires_at__gt=now,
            )
            .exclude(
                owner_id__in=blocked_ids,
            )
            .filter(
                owner__is_active=True,
            )
            .annotate(
                seen_by_user=Exists(
                    StoryView.objects.filter(
                        story=OuterRef("pk"),
                        viewer=request.user,
                    )
                )
            )
            .order_by(
                "seen_by_user",
                "-created_at",
            )
        )

        serializer = StorySerializer(
            stories,
            many=True,
            context={
                "request": request,
            },
        )

        return Response({
            "results": serializer.data,
        })


class CreateStoryView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    parser_classes = [
        MultiPartParser,
        FormParser,
    ]

    def post(self, request):
        media_id = request.data.get("media_id")
        visibility = request.data.get(
            "visibility",
            Story.Visibility.FRIENDS,
        )

        if not media_id:
            return Response(
                {"detail": "media_id is required."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        if visibility not in dict(
            Story.Visibility.choices
        ):
            return Response(
                {"detail": "Invalid visibility."},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        media = get_object_or_404(
            MediaAsset,
            id=media_id,
            owner=request.user,
            processing_status=MediaAsset.ProcessingStatus.READY,
        )

        story = Story.objects.create(
            owner=request.user,
            media=media,
            visibility=visibility,
        )

        serializer = StorySerializer(
            story,
            context={
                "request": request,
            },
        )

        return Response(
            serializer.data,
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )


class StoryDetailView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request, story_id):
        story = get_object_or_404(
            Story.objects.select_related(
                "owner",
                "media",
            ),
            id=story_id,
        )

        if story.is_expired:
            return Response(
                {"detail": "Story has expired."},
                status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            )

        if not can_view_story(
            request.user,
            story,
        ):
            return Response(
                {"detail": "You cannot view this story."},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            )

        serializer = StorySerializer(
            story,
            context={
                "request": request,
            },
        )

        return Response(serializer.data)


class MarkStoryViewedView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, story_id):
        story = get_object_or_404(
            Story,
            id=story_id,
        )

        if story.is_expired:
            return Response(
                {"detail": "Story has expired."},
                status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            )

        if not can_view_story(
            request.user,
            story,
        ):
            return Response(
                {"detail": "You cannot view this story."},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            )

        StoryView.objects.get_or_create(
            story=story,
            viewer=request.user,
        )

        return Response({
            "message": "Story marked as viewed."
        })


class DeleteStoryView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def delete(self, request, story_id):
        story = get_object_or_404(
            Story,
            id=story_id,
            owner=request.user,
        )

        story.delete()

        return Response(
            status=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT
        )
```

# 15. Story Expiration

Stories should disappear after exactly 24 hours.

The database still needs cleanup.

## NEW FILE — `backend/stories/tasks.py`

```python
from celery import shared_task
from django.utils import timezone

from .models import Story


@shared_task
def delete_expired_stories():
    deleted_count, _ = Story.objects.filter(
        expires_at__lte=timezone.now()
    ).delete()

    return deleted_count
```

---

# 16. Schedule Cleanup

If you already have Celery Beat configured, add:

## MODIFY EXISTING CELERY CONFIG

For example:

```python
from celery.schedules import crontab

CELERY_BEAT_SCHEDULE = {
    "delete-expired-stories": {
        "task": "stories.tasks.delete_expired_stories",
        "schedule": crontab(minute=0),
    },
}
```

This runs hourly.

The API already checks:

```python
expires_at__gt=timezone.now()
```

so an expired Story is unavailable immediately even if the cleanup worker has not run yet.

# 18. Story Media Rules

Part 19 already has media validation.

For Stories, use:

```text
Image
JPEG
PNG
WebP
Maximum 5 MB

Video
MP4
WebM
MOV
Maximum 100 MB
Maximum duration 180 seconds
```

Do **not** create another upload validator if your Part 19 validator already handles these rules.

Instead:

```text
Story
  ↓
MediaAsset
  ↓
Part 19 validation
  ↓
processing
  ↓
READY
  ↓
Story creation
```

This prevents duplicate media logic.

---

```python
if media.media_type not in {
    MediaAsset.MediaType.IMAGE,
    MediaAsset.MediaType.VIDEO,
}:
    return Response(
        {"detail": "Invalid story media."},
        status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
    )
```

Also verify:

```python
media.owner_id == request.user.id
```

which the existing query already does.

# 22. Story Service

## NEW FILE — `frontend/src/services/storyService.js`

```javascript
import api from "./api";

export async function getStories() {
  const response = await api.get("/stories/");
  return response.data;
}

export async function createStory(mediaId, visibility = "friends") {
  const response = await api.post("/stories/create/", {
    media_id: mediaId,
    visibility,
  });

  return response.data;
}

export async function getStory(storyId) {
  const response = await api.get(`/stories/${storyId}/`);
  return response.data;
}

export async function markStoryViewed(storyId) {
  const response = await api.post(
    `/stories/${storyId}/view/`
  );

  return response.data;
}

export async function deleteStory(storyId) {
  const response = await api.delete(
    `/stories/${storyId}/delete/`
  );

  return response.data;
}
```

---

# 23. Stories Hook

## NEW FILE — `frontend/src/hooks/useStories.js`

```javascript
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";

import {
  getStories,
  markStoryViewed,
} from "../services/storyService";

export function useStories() {
  const [stories, setStories] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);

  const loadStories = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError(null);

      const data = await getStories();

      setStories(data.results || []);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Failed to load stories:", err);

      setError(err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadStories();
  }, [loadStories]);

  const viewStory = useCallback(
    async (storyId) => {
      try {
        await markStoryViewed(storyId);

        setStories((current) =>
          current.map((story) =>
            story.id === storyId
              ? {
                  ...story,
                  is_seen: true,
                }
              : story
          )
        );
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(
          "Failed to mark story as viewed:",
          err
        );
      }
    },
    []
  );

  return {
    stories,
    loading,
    error,
    reload: loadStories,
    viewStory,
  };
}
```

# 25. Story Tray

## NEW FILE — `frontend/src/components/stories/StoryTray.jsx`

```jsx
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";

import { useStories } from "../../hooks/useStories";
import StoryViewer from "./StoryViewer";

export default function StoryTray() {
  const {
    stories,
    loading,
    reload,
    viewStory,
  } = useStories();

  const [selectedIndex, setSelectedIndex] =
    useState(null);

  const groupedStories = useMemo(() => {
    const groups = new Map();

    stories.forEach((story) => {
      const ownerId = story.owner.id;

      if (!groups.has(ownerId)) {
        groups.set(ownerId, {
          owner: story.owner,
          stories: [],
          hasUnseen: false,
        });
      }

      const group = groups.get(ownerId);

      group.stories.push(story);

      if (!story.is_seen) {
        group.hasUnseen = true;
      }
    });

    return Array.from(groups.values());
  }, [stories]);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="story-tray story-tray--loading">
        Loading stories...
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <section className="story-tray">
        <div className="story-tray__header">
          <h2>Stories</h2>
        </div>

        <div className="story-tray__items">
          {groupedStories.map((group, index) => (
            <button
              key={group.owner.id}
              type="button"
              className={
                group.hasUnseen
                  ? "story-item story-item--unseen"
                  : "story-item story-item--seen"
              }
              onClick={() => setSelectedIndex(index)}
            >
              <img
                src={group.owner.avatar}
                alt={group.owner.username}
              />

              <span>
                {group.owner.username}
              </span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      {selectedIndex !== null && (
        <StoryViewer
          groups={groupedStories}
          initialGroupIndex={selectedIndex}
          onClose={() => setSelectedIndex(null)}
          onViewed={viewStory}
          onChanged={reload}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

# 27. Story Progress

## NEW FILE — `frontend/src/components/stories/StoryProgress.jsx`

```jsx
export default function StoryProgress({
  count,
  currentIndex,
  progress,
}) {
  return (
    <div className="story-progress">
      {Array.from({ length: count }).map(
        (_, index) => (
          <div
            key={index}
            className="story-progress__item"
          >
            <div
              className="story-progress__bar"
              style={{
                width:
                  index < currentIndex
                    ? "100%"
                    : index === currentIndex
                    ? `${progress}%`
                    : "0%",
              }}
            />
          </div>
        )
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

---

# 28. Story Viewer Implementation

## NEW FILE — `frontend/src/components/stories/StoryViewer.jsx`

```jsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

import StoryProgress from "./StoryProgress";

const IMAGE_DURATION = 5000;

export default function StoryViewer({
  groups,
  initialGroupIndex,
  onClose,
  onViewed,
}) {
  const [groupIndex, setGroupIndex] =
    useState(initialGroupIndex);

  const [storyIndex, setStoryIndex] =
    useState(0);

  const [progress, setProgress] =
    useState(0);

  const group = groups[groupIndex];

  const story = useMemo(() => {
    return group?.stories?.[storyIndex];
  }, [group, storyIndex]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!story) {
      onClose();
      return;
    }

    onViewed(story.id);

    setProgress(0);

    if (story.media_type !== "image") {
      return;
    }

    const start = Date.now();

    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      const elapsed = Date.now() - start;

      const percentage =
        Math.min(
          (elapsed / IMAGE_DURATION) * 100,
          100
        );

      setProgress(percentage);

      if (percentage >= 100) {
        window.clearInterval(timer);

        goNext();
      }
    }, 50);

    return () => {
      window.clearInterval(timer);
    };
  }, [story?.id]);

  function goNext() {
    if (!group) {
      onClose();
      return;
    }

    if (
      storyIndex <
      group.stories.length - 1
    ) {
      setStoryIndex((value) => value + 1);
      return;
    }

    if (
      groupIndex <
      groups.length - 1
    ) {
      setGroupIndex((value) => value + 1);
      setStoryIndex(0);
      return;
    }

    onClose();
  }

  function goPrevious() {
    if (storyIndex > 0) {
      setStoryIndex((value) => value - 1);
      return;
    }

    if (groupIndex > 0) {
      const previousGroup =
        groups[groupIndex - 1];

      setGroupIndex((value) => value - 1);

      setStoryIndex(
        previousGroup.stories.length - 1
      );
    }
  }

  if (!story) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div className="story-viewer">
      <div className="story-viewer__top">
        <StoryProgress
          count={group.stories.length}
          currentIndex={storyIndex}
          progress={progress}
        />

        <div className="story-viewer__user">
          <img
            src={story.owner.avatar}
            alt={story.owner.username}
          />

          <span>
            {story.owner.username}
          </span>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label="Close story"
        >
          ×
        </button>
      </div>

      <button
        type="button"
        className="story-viewer__previous"
        onClick={goPrevious}
        aria-label="Previous story"
      >
        ‹
      </button>

      <div className="story-viewer__content">
        {story.media_type === "video" ? (
          <video
            src={story.media_url}
            controls
            autoPlay
            playsInline
            onTimeUpdate={(event) => {
              const video =
                event.currentTarget;

              if (video.duration) {
                setProgress(
                  (video.currentTime /
                    video.duration) *
                    100
                );
              }
            }}
            onEnded={goNext}
          />
        ) : (
          <img
            src={story.media_url}
            alt={`Story by ${story.owner.username}`}
          />
        )}
      </div>

      <button
        type="button"
        className="story-viewer__next"
        onClick={goNext}
        aria-label="Next story"
      >
        ›
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

# 29. Story Uploader

The upload flow should use the existing Part 19 media upload endpoint.

Architecture:

```text
Select file
    ↓
MediaUploader
    ↓
POST /api/media/upload/
    ↓
MediaAsset
    ↓
READY
    ↓
POST /api/stories/create/
```

This is important because Stories should not bypass your existing media validation.

---

# 30. Story Uploader

## NEW FILE — `frontend/src/components/stories/StoryUploader.jsx`

```jsx
import { useState } from "react";

import MediaUploader from "../media/MediaUploader";
import { createStory } from "../../services/storyService";

export default function StoryUploader({
  onCreated,
}) {
  const [visibility, setVisibility] =
    useState("friends");

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(false);

  async function handleUpload(media) {
    if (!media?.id) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      setLoading(true);

      await createStory(
        media.id,
        visibility
      );

      onCreated?.();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(
        "Failed to create story:",
        error
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="story-uploader">
      <h3>Create Story</h3>

      <select
        value={visibility}
        onChange={(event) =>
          setVisibility(event.target.value)
        }
      >
        <option value="friends">
          Friends
        </option>

        <option value="public">
          Public
        </option>
      </select>

      <MediaUploader
        onUploaded={handleUpload}
        accept="image/*,video/*"
        multiple={false}
      />

      {loading && (
        <p>Creating story...</p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Important

Your existing `MediaUploader` may use a different prop name such as:

```jsx
onUploadComplete
```

or:

```jsx
onUpload
```

If so, **keep your existing component API** and change only the callback name here.

Do not rewrite Part 19.

```css
  overflow: hidden;
}

.story-progress__bar {
  height: 100%;
  background: white;
  transition: width 50ms linear;
}

.story-viewer__previous,
.story-viewer__next {
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  bottom: 0;
  width: 25%;
  border: 0;
  background: transparent;
  color: transparent;
  cursor: pointer;
  z-index: 3;
}

.story-viewer__previous {
  left: 0;
}

.story-viewer__next {
  right: 0;
}
```
